# Template check - firm

**This template will not build correctly.** 1 error(s), 140 warning(s), 4 note(s).

> Redacted: slide titles and quoted text are replaced with `[redacted]`, and block ids - which are derived from those titles - with `block_01`, `block_02` and so on, consistently throughout. Every count, structural finding and slide reference is intact, so this file is safe to share outside the firm.

## Summary

| | |
|---|---|
| Template | firm |
| Library | library.pptx |
| Size | 81 MB |
| Slides | 4150 |
| Blocks | 4150 |
| Masters / layouts | 73 / 442 |
| Media files | 193 |
| Placeholders used / bound | 5 / 4 |
| Merged from | 70 decks |

Block ids came from: map (4150).

Slides were matched across decks by: bytes (4150).

## Errors (1)

### 1 placeholder(s) appear on slides but nothing fills them

`unbound-placeholder`

They will be left visible in every deck built from this template.

- `{{Form.ShortClientName}}`

## Warnings (140)

### 73 slide masters in one library

`many-masters`

Merged decks should share a master. This many usually means the source decks' masters differed byte for byte, so nothing de-duplicated - the library will be large and may look inconsistent.

### 55 conditional block(s) have no insert_after

`unanchored-block`

They are appended to the end of the deck rather than placed.

`block_120`, `block_180`, `block_300`, `block_360`, `block_600`, `block_660`, `block_839`, `block_898`, `block_1016`, `block_1134`, `block_1192`, `block_1251`, `block_1310`, `block_1369`, `block_1428`, `block_1487`, `block_1546`, `block_1605`, `block_1664`, `block_1723`, `block_1782`, `block_1841`, `block_1900`, `block_1959`, `block_2018`

### No slide was matched by creationId when the decks were merged

`weak-slide-identity`
